This roundup covers 467 Microsoft security vulnerabilities. CVSS base scores range from 4.4 to 10.0. None are reported as actively exploited at the time of release. The issues affect ASP.NET Core, Account, Active Directory Certificate Services, Active Directory Domain Services, Active Directory Federation Services, Azure AI Language, Azure Active Directory B2C, Azure Arc, Azure Cosmos DB, Azure CycleCloud, BitLocker, BranchCache, COM, Connected Devices Platform Service, Connected User Experiences and Telemetry, Copilot Studio, DNS Server, Direct Show, DirectMusic, Discovery Studio, Dynamics 365, Entra ID, Excel, Exchange Server, Fabric, GDI+, Graphic Fonts, Graphics Component, Hyper-V, IP Address Management, Install Service, Internet Connection Sharing, JScript, Local Security Authority Server, NTFS, Netlogon, Office, Office Access, Office Excel, Office PowerPoint, Office Publisher, Office Word, Outlook, Paint, Power Automate, PowerPoint, PowerShell, Raw Image Extension, Remote Access Connection Manager, Remote Desktop Client, Remote Desktop Gateway Service, Remote Desktop Services, Routing and Remote Access Service, SQL Server, SharePoint, Skype for Business, Standard XPS, Storage Port Driver, Trace Data Helper, UxTheme Library, Virtual Hard Disk, Visual Studio, Visual Studio Code, Volume Manager Driver, WebP Image Extension, Windows, Windows Ancillary Function Driver for WinSock, Windows Audio Service, Windows Bind Filter Driver, Windows Biometric Service, Windows Bluetooth Port Driver, Windows Boot Manager, Windows Broadcast DVR User Service, Windows Broker Infrastructure Service, Windows CD-ROM Driver, Windows Cloud Files Mini Filter Driver, Windows Codecs Library, Windows Connected User Experiences and Logging Service, Windows Failover Cluster, Windows Fast FAT Driver, Windows Fax Service, Windows File History Service, Windows HTTP Print Provider, Windows Hello, Windows Image Acquisition, Windows Imaging Component, Windows Installer, Windows Internet Key Exchange, Windows Kernel, Windows Key Distribution Center, Windows License Manager, Windows Link Layer Topology Discovery Protocol, Windows MIDI Service Module, Windows Management Instrumentation, Windows Media Foundation, Windows Message Queuing, Windows Modern Device Management, Windows Modern Execution Server, Windows NFS Portmapper, Windows NTFS, Windows Netlogon, Windows Network File System, Windows Overlay Filter, Windows Partition Management Driver, Windows Performance Monitor, Windows Print Spooler, Windows Program Compatibility Assistant Service, Windows Push Notifications, Windows RDP Client, Windows Remote Access Connection Manager, Windows Remote Desktop, Windows Remote Desktop Licensing Service, Windows ## CVE-2026-62916

CVE-2026-62916 is an authentication bypass vulnerability in Microsoft Entra ID arising from the use of an alternate path or channel. This vulnerability permits an unauthorized remote attacker to perform privilege escalation within the identity management environment.

Affected products:
- Entra ID

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-62916








































































































































































































































































































































































































































Related in this roundup: [CVE-2026-83941](#cve-2026-83941).

## CVE-2026-70352

CVE-2026-70352 describes a critical vulnerability in Microsoft's Azure AI Language service where a missing authentication control on a critical function allows an unauthorized remote attacker to perform privilege escalation. The vulnerability carries a CVSS base score of 10.0, indicating high severity and potential for exploitation over a network.

Affected products:
- Azure AI Language

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-70352

## CVE-2026-80098

CVE-2026-80098 is a vulnerability in Microsoft Copilot Studio involving improper verification of cryptographic signatures. This flaw allows an unauthorized attacker to perform a privilege escalation attack over a network, presenting a significant security risk given its high CVSS base score.

Affected products:
- Copilot Studio

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-80098

## CVE-2026-83711

An authorization bypass vulnerability exists in Microsoft Azure Active Directory B2C due to improper handling of user-controlled keys. An unauthenticated attacker can exploit this flaw over the network to elevate privileges, leading to a critical security impact.

Affected products:
- Azure Active Directory B2C

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-83711

## CVE-2026-62906

CVE-2026-62906 describes an improper neutralization of special elements in data query logic within Microsoft Discovery Studio. This vulnerability allows an unauthorized remote attacker to perform unauthorized data disclosure over a network, potentially exposing sensitive information due to flawed query handling.

Affected products:
- Discovery Studio

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-62906

## CVE-2026-65818

CVE-2026-65818 is a server-side request forgery (SSRF) vulnerability in Microsoft Power Automate that enables an authorized attacker to perform privilege escalation over a network.

Affected products:
- Power Automate

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-65818

## CVE-2026-69857

CVE-2026-69857 describes an authorization bypass vulnerability in Azure Cosmos DB, where a user-controlled key allows an already authorized attacker to perform network spoofing, potentially escalating access or manipulating data communication.

Affected products:
- Azure Cosmos DB

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-69857

## CVE-2026-70178

CVE-2026-70178 is a privilege escalation vulnerability in Microsoft Fabric caused by a missing authorization check, which allows an attacker with existing network access to gain unauthorized elevated privileges.

Affected products:
- Fabric

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-70178

## CVE-2026-81963

CVE-2026-81963 is a link following vulnerability in the Microsoft Windows Update Stack that allows a local attacker to escalate privileges to SYSTEM. Detection engineers should prioritize patching affected assets in accordance with CISA's BOD 26-04 guidelines.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://www.cve.org/CVERecord?id=CVE-2026-81963

- Windows

Affected products:
- Windows HTTP Print Provider

## CVE-2026-56177

CVE-2026-56177 is a use-after-free vulnerability in Windows Server that enables an already authorized local attacker to elevate their privileges. Successful exploitation allows the attacker to execute code or perform operations with higher permissions than their initial account level, posing a significant risk to system security.

Affected products:
- Windows Server

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-56177

## CVE-2026-56198

CVE-2026-56198 is an out-of-bounds read vulnerability in the Microsoft Trace Data Helper component. An attacker with local authorization can exploit this flaw to escalate privileges on the affected Windows system.

Affected products:
- Trace Data Helper

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-56198

## CVE-2026-57098

CVE-2026-57098 describes an information disclosure vulnerability in the Windows RDP Client caused by improper verification of cryptographic signatures. This flaw allows an unauthorized attacker with network access to intercept or expose sensitive data during RDP communication, presenting a risk to secure remote access sessions.

Affected products:
- Windows RDP Client

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-57098

## CVE-2026-57099

CVE-2026-57099 is a denial-of-service vulnerability in ASP.NET Core resulting from improper allocation of resources without limits or throttling. An unauthorized remote attacker can exploit this flaw to exhaust system resources, leading to service unavailability.

Affected products:
- ASP.NET Core

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-57099

## CVE-2026-58599

A heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability in the Microsoft Windows Codecs Library allows a local, unauthorized attacker to execute arbitrary code. The vulnerability is triggered by parsing malformed media files or data streams handled by the library, potentially leading to remote or local code execution.

Affected products:
- Windows Codecs Library

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-58599

## CVE-2026-67385

CVE-2026-67385 is a use-after-free vulnerability in Microsoft SQL Server that enables an authorized attacker to achieve remote code execution over a network. The vulnerability necessitates existing authorization to exploit, posing a significant risk to database availability and system integrity.

Affected products:
- SQL Server

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-67385

## CVE-2026-67388

CVE-2026-67388 is a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability in Microsoft SQL Server that allows an authenticated attacker to achieve remote code execution over a network. The vulnerability exists due to improper handling of memory during data processing, which can be exploited by an authorized user to execute arbitrary code with the privileges of the SQL Server process.

Affected products:
- SQL Server

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-67388

## CVE-2026-67631

CVE-2026-67631 is a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability in Microsoft SQL Server that allows an authenticated attacker to achieve remote code execution over a network. - SQL Server

## CVE-2026-68827

CVE-2026-68827 is an integer underflow vulnerability within the Windows GDI+ component. This flaw enables an authorized attacker to achieve privilege escalation over a network connection by triggering a wraparound condition, potentially allowing for arbitrary code execution or escalated system access.

Affected products:
- GDI+

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-68827

## CVE-2026-68828

CVE-2026-68828 is a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability within the Microsoft Remote Desktop Client. Affected products:
- Remote Desktop Client

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-68828

- Windows Kernel

## CVE-2026-68884

CVE-2026-68884 is a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability in the Windows Kernel that enables a locally authenticated attacker to perform a privilege escalation attack. Detection should focus on monitoring for anomalous kernel-mode memory access patterns or unexpected process token modifications initiated by low-privileged local user accounts.

Affected products:
- Windows Kernel

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-68884

- Standard XPS

## CVE-2026-69265

CVE-2026-69265 describes an out-of-bounds read vulnerability in the Windows NTFS driver that can be exploited by an authorized local attacker to achieve privilege escalation. - Windows

## CVE-2026-69266

CVE-2026-69266 is a vulnerability in the Windows DHCP Server component resulting from an integer overflow or wraparound condition. Affected products:
- Windows DHCP Server

Affected products:
- Windows License Manager

## CVE-2026-69282

CVE-2026-69282 is a critical vulnerability in Microsoft Office SharePoint caused by improper access control. The flaw allows an authenticated attacker to perform remote code execution (RCE) over a network, presenting a significant risk to organizational infrastructure.

- SharePoint

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-69282

## CVE-2026-69291

CVE-2026-69291 is a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability within the Windows Volume Manager Extension Driver. This vulnerability allows an unauthorized remote attacker to achieve remote code execution (RCE) on affected Windows systems via the network.

Affected products:

## CVE-2026-69309

CVE-2026-69309 is a double free vulnerability in the Windows Print Spooler components that permits a locally authenticated attacker to perform a privilege escalation attack. Detection should focus on monitoring anomalous process behaviors or memory-related crashes originating from the Print Spooler service.

Affected products:
- Windows Print Spooler

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-69309

## CVE-2026-69310

CVE-2026-69310 describes a use-after-free vulnerability in the Windows DNS component. The vulnerability allows an attacker who already has local access to the system to exploit the memory corruption to escalate their privileges, potentially gaining higher-level administrative rights on the host.

Affected products:
- Windows DNS

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-69310

- Windows Audio Service

CVE-2026-69324 is a type confusion vulnerability in the Windows Performance Monitor component. An attacker with local access can exploit this flaw to execute code or operations in a higher privilege context, resulting in local privilege escalation.

Affected products:
- Windows Performance Monitor

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-69324

## CVE-2026-69325

CVE-2026-69325 describes a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability in Microsoft JScript, which can be exploited by an unauthorized attacker to achieve remote code execution over a network. Detection efforts should focus on monitoring for anomalous JScript execution patterns or unexpected heap memory manipulation within the targeted Microsoft environment.

Affected products:
- JScript

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-69325

Affected products:
- Windows Storage

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-69328

- BranchCache

- Windows

## CVE-2026-69412

CVE-2026-69412 is a stack-based buffer overflow vulnerability in the Windows DHCP Server component. An authorized attacker on an adjacent network can exploit this flaw to achieve remote code execution on the target server. Detection should focus on anomalous traffic patterns or unexpected process execution originating from the DHCP service.

Affected products:
- Windows DHCP Server

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69412

## CVE-2026-69415

CVE-2026-69415 describes a vulnerability in the Windows DHCP Server component where a lack of authentication for a critical function enables an authorized network-based attacker to perform privilege escalation. Detection engineers should monitor for unauthorized or unusual administrative activity originating from DHCP service accounts or unexpected attempts to interact with the DHCP server management functions by non-privileged entities.

Affected products:
- Windows DHCP Server

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69415

## CVE-2026-69448

CVE-2026-69448 describes a race condition vulnerability in the Windows Bluetooth Service that enables an authorized local attacker to elevate their privileges. Detection engineers should monitor for unauthorized modifications to system-level Bluetooth service processes or suspicious activity occurring within the context of the Bluetooth service account.

Affected products:

- Windows

- Windows DNS Server

- Remote Desktop Services

- Windows

- Windows Error Reporting

## CVE-2026-69627

CVE-2026-69627 is an out-of-bounds read vulnerability in the Windows Remote Desktop Licensing Service. An authorized, locally authenticated attacker could exploit this vulnerability to disclose sensitive information from the service's memory space.

Affected products:
- Windows Remote Desktop Licensing Service

## CVE-2026-69628

CVE-2026-69628 is a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability within the Windows iSCSI component that allows an authenticated attacker to achieve remote code execution over a network. Detection should focus on monitoring anomalous iSCSI traffic patterns and unexpected child processes spawning from system services associated with storage protocols.

Affected products:
- Windows iSCSI

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69628

## CVE-2026-69790

CVE-2026-69790 is a local elevation of privilege vulnerability in the Windows Credential Providers component caused by a heap-based buffer overflow. An authenticated attacker can exploit this flaw to gain higher privileges on the target system.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69790

## CVE-2026-69693

A use-after-free vulnerability in the Windows Device Association Broker service allows a locally authenticated attacker to elevate their privileges to a higher integrity level. This vulnerability requires the attacker to already have access to the system to exploit the flaw, typically targeting the service's memory management to achieve privilege escalation.

Affected products:
- Windows Device Association Broker service

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69693

## CVE-2026-69729

CVE-2026-69729 is a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability within Windows Credential Providers that enables an authorized attacker to achieve remote code execution over a network. Detection should focus on monitoring abnormal process execution behavior initiated by system-level credential handling services.

Affected products:
- Windows Credential Providers

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69729

## CVE-2026-69717

CVE-2026-69717 is a privilege escalation vulnerability in Windows Group Policy caused by an untrusted pointer dereference. An attacker with authorized access can exploit this flaw to elevate their privileges over the network.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69717

## CVE-2026-69862

CVE-2026-69862 is an out-of-bounds read vulnerability in the Windows Wireless Wide Area Network Service that enables an authenticated local attacker to perform information disclosure. Detection engineers should monitor for unusual access patterns or crashes associated with the wwansvc service.

Affected products:
- Windows Wireless Wide Area Network Service

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69862

## CVE-2026-69832

CVE-2026-69832 is an information disclosure vulnerability in the Windows Win32k component that allows a local, authorized attacker to gain access to sensitive system information due to improper handling of information in the control sphere.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69832

## CVE-2026-69839

CVE-2026-69839 describes a denial of service vulnerability in the Windows iSCSI Target Service caused by an uncaught exception. An authorized attacker can exploit this remotely to crash the service, resulting in a loss of availability for iSCSI storage resources.

Affected products:
- Windows iSCSI Target Service

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69839

## CVE-2026-69772

CVE-2026-69772 describes a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability in the Windows Network File System (NFS) component. An unauthorized remote attacker can leverage this vulnerability to execute arbitrary code on an affected system via network requests.

Affected products:
- Windows Network File System

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69772

## CVE-2026-69775

CVE-2026-69775 is a use-after-free vulnerability in the Windows Desktop Window Manager (DWM) Core Library that allows an authenticated attacker to perform local or network-based privilege escalation.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69775

## CVE-2026-69758

A heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability in the Windows Universal Disk Format File System Driver (UDFS) allows a locally authenticated attacker to achieve privilege escalation. This vulnerability represents a kernel-level security flaw impacting the Windows storage driver subsystem.

Affected products:
- Windows Universal Disk Format File System Driver

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69758

## CVE-2026-69771

CVE-2026-69771 is a security feature bypass vulnerability in the Windows Container Manager Service. It is caused by improper link resolution before file access, which allows an authorized local attacker to perform unauthorized file operations, potentially leading to security feature bypass.

Affected products:
- Windows Container Manager Service

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69771

## CVE-2026-69822

A numeric truncation vulnerability exists in the Windows Kerberos implementation, which can be exploited by an authenticated local attacker to gain elevated privileges.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69822

## CVE-2026-69732

CVE-2026-69732 is a heap-based buffer overflow vulnerability in the Windows Link Layer Topology Discovery (LLTD) Protocol. An unauthenticated attacker can exploit this via the network to achieve remote code execution on affected Windows systems.

Affected products:
- Windows Link Layer Topology Discovery Protocol

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69732

## CVE-2026-69770

CVE-2026-69770 is an information disclosure vulnerability in the Windows Spaceport.sys driver. The flaw is caused by the use of an uninitialized resource, which may allow an authorized local attacker to read sensitive information from memory.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69770

- Windows Imaging Component

## CVE-2026-69712

CVE-2026-69712 is a remote code execution vulnerability in the Windows Key Distribution Center service stemming from a use-after-free flaw. An authorized attacker can leverage this vulnerability to execute arbitrary code over the network, potentially leading to system compromise.

Affected products:
- Windows Key Distribution Center

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69712

## CVE-2026-69676

CVE-2026-69676 is a remote code execution vulnerability in Windows Kerberos caused by an authentication bypass via a capture-replay attack. An authorized attacker can leverage this flaw to execute arbitrary code over the network by capturing and replaying authentication traffic.

Affected products:
- Windows

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-69676

CVE-2026-70584 is a type confusion vulnerability within the Windows Core Messaging component that enables a locally authenticated attacker to escalate their privileges. Affected products:
- Windows Core Messaging

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70584

## CVE-2026-70585

CVE-2026-70585 is a use-after-free vulnerability in the Windows Services for NFS ONCRPC XDR Driver. An authenticated attacker can exploit this flaw to achieve local remote code execution by triggering memory corruption within the driver's handling of XDR data structures.

Affected products:
- Windows Services for NFS

- Paint

Affected products:
- Windows Remote Desktop Protocol

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-70587

- Windows

- Windows NTFS

## CVE-2026-72978

A denial of service vulnerability exists in Microsoft Active Directory Federation Services due to improper resource allocation and a lack of request throttling. An unauthenticated remote attacker can exploit this flaw by sending specially crafted requests to the service, causing it to consume excessive resources and become unresponsive, thereby impacting the availability of authentication services.

Affected products:
- Active Directory Federation Services

Source: https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-72978

## CVE-2026-77495

- Windows Imaging Component
